### Assistant

_source line 43_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the full picture. Let me also check the local version to see the fix that was applied.

Key findings from reading the code:

1. **Save mechanism**: `save_sampled_results` at line 670-673 writes JSONL with `json.dumps(inst, ensure_ascii=False)`. The crash happens at line 673 because `ensure_ascii=False` + lone surrogates from vLLM = UnicodeEncodeError when writing to a UTF-8 file.

2. **No intermediate checkpoint**: The pipeline is:
   - `run_sampling_pipeline()` does ALL inference (stochastic + greedy) in memory
   - At the end, calls `save_sampled_results()` once to write all results
   - No intermediate saves, no pickle/cache of inference results

3. **The fix**: The user mentioned `errors='replace'` was deployed to the source file at line 673, but `grep -n "errors" sampling.py` returned nothing - so the fix isn't actually in the deployed version on the server. Let me re-read what the user said about the local copy versus the server state.

The user said the fix was deployed to the source file, but the grep search shows no `errors=` anywhere in the current file. This could mean the fix exists in the local repository but hasn't been synced to the server yet, or the running process is still using cached old code in memory even though the file was updated.

Looking at line 673 on the server - it's writing JSON with `ensure_ascii=False`, which means `json.dumps` will try to output actual Unicode characters instead of escape sequences. If there are lone surrogates in the data, these invalid Unicode characters will cause an encoding error when the file tries to write them.

The solution is to add `errors='replace'` to the `open()` call, which would swap out those problematic surrogates with a replacement character instead of crashing.
```

## Save 机制分析

**架构**：全部推理结果存在内存，最后一次性写文件。

流程：
1. `run_sampling_pipeline()` → 在内存中完成所有 stochastic sampling (N=32) + greedy sampling (N=1)
2. 结果组装到 `sampled_instances: list[dict]` 列表
3. 最后调用 `save_sampled_results(sampled_instances, output_path)` 一次性写 JSONL
4. **无任何 intermediate checkpoint**——没有 pickle、没有 partial save、没有 batch flush

**Crash 点**：`sampling.py:673`
```python
with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    for inst in sampled:
        f.write(json.dumps(inst, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
```
`ensure_ascii=False` + vLLM 生成的 lone surrogates → UTF-8 编码器拒绝 → `UnicodeEncodeError`。

## 关键发现：fix 并未部署

服务器上的 sampling.py **没有** `errors='replace'`——`grep -n "errors" sampling.py` 返回空。文件仍是原始的 `open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8")`。运行中的 N=32 进程加载的代码 100% 是有漏洞的版本。

|------|------|
| N=32 save 会 crash 吗？ | **高概率会。** N=16 在 72% save 位置就撞到了 surrogate。N=32 每个 instance 有 2x 更多 samples，遇到 surrogate 的概率更高。 |
| Partial output 可用吗？ | **可用。** 写到 crash 之前的行都是完整有效的 JSONL（每行一个完整 json.dumps 输出）。可以用 `load_sampled_results()` 直接加载。 |
| 能否跳过推理只恢复 save？ | **不行。** 没有中间 pickle/cache。推理结果只存在于 `run_sampling_pipeline()` 的局部变量 `sampled_instances` 里。UnicodeEncodeError 导致进程退出，内存丢失。 |
| 能否热补丁？ | **不可行。** Python bytecode 已编译进内存，修改磁盘文件不影响运行中的进程。理论上可以 gdb attach + py-call 替换函数对象，但风险极高，可能直接 crash 进程。 |
| 完全重跑需要多久？ | 11h / 0.7 ≈ **15.7h 总推理时间**。剩余推理 ~4.7h，加上推理后的 greedy pass + parse + save。 |

## 推荐方案

**继续跑，不要杀进程。同时做两件准备：**

1. **立即修复磁盘上的 sampling.py**，把 line 672 改为：
   ```python
   with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
   ```

2. **准备 rescue 脚本**：N=32 推理完成后（~4.7h），save 大概率 crash。此时：
   - 统计 partial JSONL 已写入行数 → 这些 instance 数据完好
   - 用 `--skip_sampling --samples_path &lt;partial_file>` 先跑 evaluation（拿到部分结果）
   - 如果需要完整数据：用 `--start_index <已保存数> --end_index &lt;total>` 只对缺失 instance 重跑推理（磁盘上已是修复版代码），然后拼接两个 JSONL

**不建议杀掉重跑**的原因：已投入 ~11h；让它跑完后 partial output 至少覆盖 ~70%+ instance（参考 N=16 经验），这些数据可以直接用于评估。最坏情况下只需补跑剩余 ~30% instance（~4-5h），比完全重跑（~15.7h）节省大量时间。

## Save 机制分析

**架构**：全部推理结果存在内存，最后一次性写文件。

流程：
1. `run_sampling_pipeline()` → 在内存中完成所有 stochastic sampling (N=32) + greedy sampling (N=1)
2. 结果组装到 `sampled_instances: list[dict]` 列表
3. 最后调用 `save_sampled_results(sampled_instances, output_path)` 一次性写 JSONL
4. **无任何 intermediate checkpoint**——没有 pickle、没有 partial save、没有 batch flush

**Crash 点**：`sampling.py:673`
```python
with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    for inst in sampled:
        f.write(json.dumps(inst, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
```
`ensure_ascii=False` + vLLM 生成的 lone surrogates → UTF-8 编码器拒绝 → `UnicodeEncodeError`。

## 关键发现：fix 并未部署

服务器上的 sampling.py **没有** `errors='replace'`——`grep -n "errors" sampling.py` 返回空。文件仍是原始的 `open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8")`。运行中的 N=32 进程加载的代码 100% 是有漏洞的版本。
